## Run 40153720103 - 2026-10-25T02:15Z (failure-triage wake: the single RC sweep failed on macOS only in a known frame-capture race; fresh survey 02:15-02:25Z) - ONE FIX ISSUE + ONE REPORT PING; NO RC RE-SWEEP

Run link: https://github.com/Userfrom1995/RandomLabs/actions/runs/40153720103

**The wake**: a `workflow_run` failure notification for `maze-chase-release` run 40153483529 (the single RC sweep dispatched by the PR-close maintainer run 40153200602 at 02:11:09Z on `22ed34d9`, created 02:11:09Z, completed failure 02:14:48Z). The notification is not an operator request; the staged `RUNNER_TEMP` prompt is the authoritative edition.

**State snapshot (verified 02:15-02:25Z; live repo via `gh`; `origin/main` = `22ed34d9`; workspace HEAD `22ed34d9` on the CLI dispatch branch `opencode/dispatch-975489-20261025021505`, tree clean, `.maintainer/` gitignored; no branch command run, nothing pushed)**:
- **The RC sweep 40153483529: Linux and Windows green, macOS failed, publish skipped, no tag/release.** Linux job 121049745900 SUCCESS 02:14:47Z (6,770,226-byte `maze-chase-linux` artifact uploaded); Windows job 121049746009 SUCCESS 02:14:21Z (3,093,852-byte `maze-chase-windows` artifact uploaded); macOS job 121049746115 FAILURE 02:13:17Z in `Workspace tests and deterministic verifier` (`-p maze-chase-terminal --test tester_dynamic_layout_hostile`, `pixel_reports_shape_footprint_and_raster` panicked at `crates/terminal/tests/tester_dynamic_layout_hostile.rs:571` with `first board frame`; the binary reported 4 passed, 1 failed); publish job 121050443998 skipped. Verified: `git ls-remote --tags` has no `maze-chase-v*` tag, and `gh release list` still shows only Desktop Pet 1.5.0. The three #760 repairs held: the Linux XKB dependency, the Windows smoke `$Version`/`$reported` fix, and the macOS reduced-motion assertion all passed. #657 stays open for the stable `1.0.0`.
- **The macOS failure is a harness race, not a product regression.** The test's PNG dims (560x620) and placement ((31,56)) assertions PASS before the panic, so the shipped binary emitted the kitty transmit and placement correctly. `Session::wait_frame_closed()` searches the whole accumulated buffer for `ESC[?2026l`; the `pixel_reports_*` session never clears the buffer before `reach_playing`, so it can match a title-screen frame close and return while the playing frame is still open; `frame_containing()` then sees no complete frame with the placement. This is the same tail-only race already documented and fixed in `tester_render_width_hostile.rs` (`capture_frame` / `last_complete_frame`, about 1 in 3 runs). The app composes the whole frame and writes it with one `write_all` (`render_frame` plus the loop's `stdout.write_all`), and Linux passes the same test.

- **Root cause (verified myself, not taken from the log).** macOS `/bin/bash` is 3.2.57. I downloaded and built pristine bash-3.2.57 and reproduced the exact parse failure: bash 3.2.57 cannot parse ANY `case ... ;; esac` inside a `$( ... )` command substitution. `y=$(case x in a) echo hi ;; esac)` fails; the same `case` outside a substitution parses; an `if` inside the substitution parses; a function defined outside and called inside parses; `;&` is unsupported too (bash 4 feature). The failing line is `opencode-peros-test.yml:207` (`case "$dir" in "$HOME"|"$HOME"/*) continue ;; esac`); the sibling mode validation at `:213` would fail next. The Linux copy (`:658`/`:664`) survives on bash 5 and the Windows copy (`:1166`/`:1172`) takes the grep branch, so only macOS is hit. Every one of the last 200 `opencode-peros-test` runs is skip-only except this failure, so the macOS branch of this step had never executed successfully since the #713 round-18/19 trusted-path hardening landed. This is a latent workflow portability defect, first exposed by the first macOS execution.

Run link: https://github.com/Userfrom1995/RandomLabs/actions/runs/40235507720

**The wake**: an `issue_comment` created event on PR #766. The binding Quality Council eval run 40232601861 completed at 21:26:24Z with decision `fix` (9.6/10), and its owner-authored rejection comment (6403521933) plus the PAT `/oc maintainer` handoff (21:26:33Z) summoned this run. **Council verdict (verified from the PR thread and run 40232601861)**: decision `fix`, score 9.6/10. The Council independently reproduced the #765 waveform fix and the DPR, resize, and normal-load repairs: a normal load at 1440x900 DPR 1 paints 38,102 waveform pixels plus a 72-pixel playhead, playback advances and repaints, a 50% canvas click seeks to 77.9 s with the head at 441.5 px (expected 441.43), DPR 2 and DPR 3 geometry are exact, the mobile selftest passes with 115,198 waveform pixels and no overflow, `origin/main` reproduces the blank 300x72 pre-fix state, the focused suite is 11/11, `bash thunderline/repro.sh` is green (94 tests), and all four functional mutants are caught. One blocking defect remains.

**Blocking finding (exact)**: `thunderline/player/player.js:232-235` keeps the zero-width fallback `var w = canvas.clientWidth || canvas.width;` followed by `canvas.width = Math.round(w * dpr);`. With a malformed `song.json`, the audio decodes, the error card hides the transport, and the new guard (which no longer requires `peaks.length`) lets `drawOverview()` run with `clientWidth === 0`; `w` then reads the device-pixel backing store and the next line multiplies it by DPR again. Verified on this head at DPR 2: eight resizes grow the canvas 600, 2,400, 9,600, 38,400, 153,600, 614,400, 2,457,600, 9,830,400 px while `origin/main` stays at 300x72. Required fix: return before touching the bitmap when `canvas.clientWidth === 0`, add a regression test (malformed `song.json`, error card, repeated DPR-2 resizes, assert no growth beyond the default), and pin the two surviving test gaps: a changed column count must recompute peaks (the `if (!peaks.length)` cache mutant must fail) and an empty peaks array must fail the selftest (reverting the assertion to `typeof peaks !== "undefined"` must fail). Non-blocking notes (recorded, not dispatched): the stale "17 checks" display versus 21 evaluated; no `aria-valuenow` on the slider role; the 72 px coordinate space versus the 70 px border-box content; the stale PR body; driver temp-profile cleanup and stdout diagnostics; and the base having advanced to `74432613` (.github only) while the PR stays MERGEABLE/CLEAN.
